Kochi supply issues shift orders to Colombo

East Coast ports face operational challenges

    India's bunker fuel demand remained subdued in September
and has continued to soften into October, as higher very low
sulfur fuel oil prices relative to key regional hubs such as
Singapore and Fujairah weighed on inquiries across major ports,
market participants said.

    "October has started on a dull note, with inquiry remaining
very limited," a Dubai-based trader for the Indian market told
Platts, part of S&P Global Energy, Oct. 8. "September was
relatively quiet, with volumes lower across grades compared
with previous months. The first half was moderately active, but
weakened significantly in the second half."

    "We were previously seeing around five to six inquiries a day
on average, but this has now dropped to just one or two," the
trader said. "On the West Coast, most inquiries have been seen

    The paper VLSFO market rebounded after a weaker start to
October, with market participants seeing some arbitrage volumes
leaving the region.

    With a well-supplied European VLSFO market and wider East-
West paper spreads, the arbitrage has become more economic.

Hormuz shipping traffic rises Oct. 7 but remains
below September weekly averages

Hormuz traffic rises to 23 ships Oct. 7

Tanker suffers attack near Qatar coastline

7-day averages below previous transit volumes

    Traffic through the Strait of Hormuz edged up Oct. 7
to 23 transits from 19 the previous day, but 7-day transit
averages remain below levels seen throughout most of
September, according to the latest report from S&P Global
Commodities At Sea.

    In the first week of October, the average transit count was
22 per day, compared with 27 ships transiting the contested
waterway per day on average in the final week of September.
However, transit levels are slightly higher than the first week of
September when transits averaged around 19 vessels each day.

    A resurgence of ship attacks has kept traffic through the

Platts revises measurement units, descriptions for FO Licht
ethanol prices

    The following Platts FOLicht ethanol prices have had
their units of measurement and descriptions updated, as
summarized below.

    Cubic Meter Pure Alcohol (CPA)
    · Type: Volume Unit
    · Definition: A unit of volume that measures the volume of
pure (100%) alcohol. One CBMPA equals 1 cubic meter (1,000
liters) of pure alcohol. Unlike a standard cubic meter (m³), which
measures the total volume of any substance (liquid, gas, or solid),
CBMPA refers specifically to the ethanol content, not the total
liquid volume.
